- **get-default-access-controls-least-privilege**: `IAgentFactory.GetDefaultAccessControls` fills a temporary `Access Control Buffer` used when an instance is created. Permission sets that exist only as user-created sets in a sandbox are missing in the next environment. Granting `D365 BUS FULL ACCESS` or SUPER gives the agent a user-sized blast radius. Effective rights are still the intersection with the assigning user's permissions. ([microsoft/BCQuality](https://github.com/microsoft/BCQuality/blob/HEAD/community/knowledge/agents/get-default-access-controls-least-privilege.md) · ★213)
- **get-default-profile-lives-in-the-app**: `IAgentFactory.GetDefaultProfile` assigns the Role Center and page customizations the agent UI-navigates. ([microsoft/BCQuality](https://github.com/microsoft/BCQuality/blob/HEAD/community/knowledge/agents/get-default-profile-lives-in-the-app.md) · ★213)

- **glossary**: - **可信回答**：由 Core 呈现的问题绑定到同宿主、呈现之后、唯一且未消费的用户事件；原生表单回答在同一次调用内以同一 interaction 落账。 - **执行授权依据**：决定是否允许进入实现的稳定语义摘要，包含 scope、执行相关任务/RU、文件范围、恢复语义和当前阻断风险，不包含 review batch id 或阶段投影。 - **证据失效**：已记录的验证、Trace、Review 或 checkpoint 不再满足其引用依据时的显式 stale 状态；失效不会被自动当作风险接受。 - **工作区归属**：路径是否属于当前 feature 的用户结论。已观察不等于已归属；未知路径必须通过批量或逐个 interaction 处理。 - **宿主接线健康**：Claude/Codex adapter 在项目级记录的 SessionStart 或 UserPromptSu ([wxy-hh/dev-flow](https://github.com/wxy-hh/dev-flow/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/glossary.md) · ★2)
